# GLORIOUS TAKLUNG KAGYU INTERNATIONAL GREAT ASPIRATION PRAYER Arranged for Recitation ^0

## 1. Refuge, Bodhicitta, Invocation, Blessing the Foundation, and so on. ^1-0

I and all sentient beings as limitless as the sky go for refuge to the essence of all the Tathagatas of the three times and the ten directions’ body, speech, mind, qualities and activity, the source of all the 84,000 collections of Dharma, ^1-1

the lord of all the noble Sangha, to the the all the glorious, compassionate, root and lineage Gurus! ^1-2

I go for refuge to all the assembled mandalas of the Yidams! ^1-3

I go for refuge to all Buddhas, the accomplished transcendant conquerors! ^1-4

I go for refuge to the holy Dharma! ^1-5

I go for refuge to all the noble Sangha! ^1-6

I go for refuge to all Dakas, Dakinis, Dharmapalas and Guards endowed with the eyes of original awareness! ^1-7

To the Buddha, Dharma, and all the Supreme Assembly,
I go for refuge until Enlightenment.
By the merit of my generosity and so forth,
My I attain Buddhahood for the benefit of beings. ^1-8

May all sentient beings be endowed with happiness and the causes of happiness! ^1-9

May they be free from suffering and the causes of suffering! ^1-10

May they never be separated from the sacred bliss that is free from suffering! ^1-11

May they abide in the great equanimity free of attachment and aversion to near and far! ^1-12

By the truth of the Three Rare and Supreme Ones, and ^1-13

by the power of all the completely pure Buddha Realms of Buddhas and Bodhisattvas who have completed the Pure Realm training and so on, ^1-14

by the power of our devotion, may this place be adorned with all the Buddha Realms and perfectly endowed with all qualities! ^1-15

May the ground be pure everywhere,
with no pebbles or sharp objects,
level like the palm of the hand,
naturally smooth and composed of Lapis Lazuli. ^1-16

May pleasure groves of wish-fulfilling trees,
with all the Buddhas and Bodhisattvas,
filled with the sweet sound of the Dharma,
pervade everywhere in all directions! ^1-17

Although the surrounding mandala may decline,
along with many pure Bodhisattvas,
may my own excellent qualities
continually abide as a beautiful ornament! ^1-18

May all embodied beings, birds and so forth,
the pure realm and all the rays of light,
extending even beyond space, continually resound
with the unceasing song of Dharma! ^1-19

HUNG! Protecting all sentient beings without exception,
Unconquered Deity who destroys the heaped race of Maras,
Who knows all Reality just as it is,
Please come to this place with your retinue, Accomplished Conqueror! ^1-20

Bhagavan, for many countless aeons,
Purifying compassion out of love for beings,
Completely perfecting your ancient vast aspiration,
Because of your intention to benefit all beings, ^1-21

from the natural palace of the Dharmakaya,
bestowing blessings and displaying manifold miracles,
so that all sentient beings may be ultimately liberated,
Total Purity, together with your retinue, please come here! ^1-22

Lord, who is powerful over all Dharmas,
shining like refined pure gold,
with vast radiance greater than the sun,
by sincerely gazing with the eyes of faith, ^1-23

peaceful, endowed with great compassion,
disciplined, abiding in the meditative states,
free of attachment to phenomena and awareness,
all endowed with unconquerable strength, ^1-24

Come here! Come here, you deities of this place!
Omniscient supreme conqueror,
Reflected image of extremely virtuous deeds,
Please come to this place of offerings! ^1-25

Here and now, together with your form,
We pray that you not only abide for the benefit of beings,
but bestow power over lifespan and freedom from illness,
and bestow every excellence! ^1-26

Compassionate conqueror, it is excellent that you have come!
We are all endowed with merit and great fortune!
Along with accepting my offering substances,
Please abide here and now! ^1-27

In the pollen heart of an eight-petalled lotus,
the size of all the billionfold universe,
expanding the offerings with bliss,
we pray you will blissfully abide here! ^1-28

Just as when the Buddha was born,
the gods offered him a bath,
and purified Him with divine water,
in that same way, I offer ablution, ^1-29

with this supreme purifying substance,
the unsurpassable water of compassion.
By the blessings of this water of pristine awareness,
Please bestow the accomplishment of whatever is desired! ^1-30

I invite all the Tathagatas and their Bodhisattva children:
Come bathe yourselves in scented water,
poured from overflowing vases made of beautiful jewels,
all while accompanied by beautiful music! ^1-31

Although the Buddha’s body, speech and mind are not obscured with afflictive emotions,
In order to purify the stains of sentient beings’ body, speech and mind,
by the Buddha’s accepting this bathing of their body, speech and mind,
May the stains of sentient beings’ body, speech and mind be purified! ^1-32

Then let me dry you with incomparable cloth,
Pure and anointed with beautiful perfume,
and dress you in the most excellent of garments,
lightly scented and dyed in vivid colours, ^1-33

I offer clothing made of the finest fabrics,
and hundreds of the most beautiful ornaments,
to grace the bodies of noble Samantabhadra, Manjughosha,
Lokeshvara and all the rest, ^1-34

with the softest and most luxurious divine raiment!
Through my offering to your pure unchangeable vajra
body with undivided faith,
may I also attain the vajra body! ^1-35

These clothes, multicolored like a rainbow,
that produce bliss whenever they are touched,
I offer in order to purify my own mind.
May I be adorned with the sublime clothes of patience! ^1-36

May a multitude of other offerings,
with sweet music, beautiful to hear,
be arrayed in great successive clouds
to dispel the sufferings of sentient beings! ^1-37

## 2. The Seven Branches Prayer ^2-0

Homage to the Noble Youthful Manjushri! ^2-1

All Lions of Men, Sugatas of the Three Times
who abide in the ten directions of the world,
to all of them, without exception, I myself
sincerely pay homage with body, speech, and mind! ^2-2

By the power of this aspiration prayer of virtuous conduct,
actually perceiving all Victorious Ones with my mind,
bowing with bodies as numerous as the atoms of the Pure Realms,
I pay homage to all the Victorious Ones! ^2-3

Upon each atom are as many Buddhas [again] as those atoms,
abiding among the Buddha’s Children [the Bodhisattvas].
In that way, all the Expanse of Reality without exception,
I imagine it will be filled with all Victorious Ones! ^2-4

All of them are praised as an inexhaustible ocean,
with the sound of this melodious ocean,
supremely expressing the enlightened qualities of all Victorious Ones!
I praise all the Sugatas [ones gone to bliss]! ^2-5

[With] sacred flowers, sacred garlands, and
all musical instruments [cymbals], perfume, the supreme parasol,
and supreme butter lamps, and sacred incense,
I make offerings to all Victorious Ones! ^2-6

All sacred garments and supreme fragrances,
and vessels of aromatic powder equal to the Supreme Mountain [Meru],
and with all these exalted, supreme, and specifically arranged offerings,
I offer to all the Victorious Ones! ^2-7

Whatever offerings there are, unsurpassable and vast,
all of those [are offered] to all Victorious Ones with devotion!
By the power of confidence in this [aspiration of] virtuous conduct,
I prostrate and offer to all Victorious Ones! ^2-8

Under the power of attachment, hatred and ignorance,
[through] body, and speech, and likewise mind,
whatever evil actions I have committed,
I confess all of them individually. ^2-9

All the Victorious Ones of the ten direction and the Buddha’s Children,
Pratyekabuddhas, those on the [Paths of] Training and No [More] Training, and-
whatever merit [gathered] by all animate beings,
I sympathetically rejoice in all of it! ^2-10

Whoever may be as burning lamps for the ten directions of this perishable universe,
[those who] attain the Enlightened Stages of Buddhahood without attachment,
all of those Protectors, I myself-
entreat to turn the Unsurpassable Wheel [of Dharma]! ^2-11

To all of those who demonstrate the state of Passing Beyond Sorrow [Nirvana],
in order to bring benefit and happiness to all animate beings,
remain for as many aeons as there are atoms in the Pure Realms!
I entreat you with my hands tightly folded in prayer! ^2-12

[By] prostrate homage, and confessing with offerings, and-
sympathetic rejoicing, and supplicating exhortation,
whatever little merit I have managed to accumulate,
I dedicate all of it to Enlightenment! ^2-13

## 3. The Fifteen Dharma Practices of the Taklung ^3-0

### The King of Dharma ^3-1-0

Dharma King, protector of all beings as limitless as space,
who has attained the state of unified non-learning,
manifesting the illusory net with great love,
I pray to Glorious TaklungThangpa! ^3-1

Guiding all beings to liberation by showing his stainless temporary face,
showing the temple of his supreme form of the completely pure three secrets,
Glorious Guidance on his throne of unchanging rainbow light
I prostrate to the pure appearance of the Three Kayas of Great Bliss! ^3-2

Accomplished Transcendent Conqueror in the midst of the stainless Sangha,
innate inseparability of expanse and original awareness,
who looks on all beings with eyes of great compassion,
I prostrate to Varahi’s self appearing Four Kayas! ^3-3

From the expanse of nonconceptual compassion, with skilled methods,
although abiding in the single taste of unelaborate great bliss,
manifesting the levels of the Command Lineage to guide through existence and peace,
I prostrate to Indrabhuti and his retinue! ^3-4

Solitary hero who manifests the union of bliss and emptiness,
natural wisdom, Vajra Yogini,
the Guide Kurukulle who accomplishes all goals,
I prostrate to the Six Excellent Precious Dharma Guards! ^3-5

By praising the Six Deities, the cause of the Seven Founders of the Kagyu,
may all beings, through stainless virtue,
like finding a precious jewel, inseparable from perfection,
unite the Three Qualities in one activity! ^3-6

<small>This was spoken by the Dharmakaya Sangye Yarjon.</small> ^3-7

Dharmakaya of the Sugatas of the three times,
I pray to you, look with the eyes of compassion!
With no obstacles to Dharma from dependent phenomena,
I pray for the blessing of natural independent activity of the holy Dharma. ^3-8

In a solitary place free from danger and the three distractions,
completely abandoning all worldly entanglements without exception,
accomplishing the essence of the holy Dharma in one lifetime,
bless me quickly! Look with eyes of compassion! ^3-9

Perverted guides of the body, speech and mind, and
all obstacles from an undevoted mind of the three obscurations,
By the unlimited power of the Four Karmas,
I pray for the blessing that they all be pacified and purified without exception! ^3-10

All memories, thoughts, karma, and everything I have done,
without being clothed by the stain of non-Dharma,
to completely purify and naturally transform them into Dharma,
I pray for the blessing of your precious power. ^3-11

My own mind is primordially unborn and completely pure.
Without fearing temporary conditions, attaining realization,
resting balanced in the state of the luminous Dharmakaya,
I pray for the blessing of completely pure causes and results. ^3-12

accomplishing the pure Four Karmas effortlessly and naturally,
Introducing that to whomever is to be taught,
unceasingly fulfilling the hopes of all disciples,
I pray you bestow blessings of freedom from all doubt! ^3-13

<small>The above was spoken by Lord Sangye Yarjon.</small> ^3-14

Completely exhausting all faults,
transforming them into the treasure of all good qualities,
and completing all final results,
I prostrate to all Gurus! ^3-15

Having departed to the state of Dharmata,
though you never move from the state of Dharmakaya,
all of us, disciples, attendants, the six kinds of beings,
without forgetting our former commitments, ^3-16

gathered in clouds by your great compassion,
experiencing bliss without any attachment,
the reality of luminous clarity with no attachment,
I pray that you send a great rain of uninterrupted, ^3-17

supreme, stable awareness!
Though you never move from the state of non-arising,
may all the thoughts, happiness and suffering of myself
and all following sentient beings, ^3-18

all be completely pacified in the river of equanimity,
compassion and love,
and be experienced as Great Bliss.
Though you abide within the state of the experience of non-appearance, ^3-19

we pray that, by all of our supplications,
may whatever any of us wish for,
all our aims, be meaningfully accomplished.
Noble one, even though you have realized ^3-20

that characteristics of things are destroyed,
through clearly knowing the three times,
and may all those who are engaged in accomplishment,
be held closely in your heart, we pray. ^3-21

Completely pacifying and exhausting all elaborations,
may we, disciples who follow your command,
so far as we follow after you,
have all our faults purified and qualities completed! ^3-22

Although understanding that happiness and suffering are the same,
by overpowering the suffering of aches and pains,
and establishing beings in happiness,
I pray you may pacify all sorrow! ^3-23

throughout the three times without exception,
although the characteristics of distractions are pacified,
may all those who practice following your example,
when looking undistractedly ^3-24

at the essential nature of mind,
attain independence through the power of investigating
in a forest solitude!
like a mother thinking of her children, ^3-25

through your great compassion,
may I and all beings without exception,
be permanently liberated from illness and suffering,
and be blessed to attain omniscience! ^3-26

Through the blessings of the Precious Lord,
may I and all limitless sentient beings
be filled to overflowing
with the original awareness of the Tathagatas, ^3-27

and be ripened to consummation, we pray!
So that we all might accomplish the rare
and supreme yidams and dharmapalas,
completely pacify all obstructors, ^3-28

and attain supreme realization itself,
we pray that you bestow vast blessings!
May the flowing river of love not be blocked,
and the roots of compassion be kept watered, ^3-29

the green tree of Bodhicitta spread widely
and the flowers of experience and realization bloom,
so that the fruit of the two benefits may ripen!
Rare and supreme Gurus and Yidams, ^3-30

Dharmapalas, Dakinis of the virtuous side,
when we all become distracted,
when in isolated retreat, helplessly drawn
by desire and clinging to outward appearances, ^3-31

by understanding they are all like dreams,
please transform all inner grasping,
attachment, and desire into bliss!
All hateful thoughts that arise ^3-32

are transformed into compassion.
All ignorance, drowsiness, and sinking
transform into the wisdom of unmanifest luminosity,
like snow falling on the surface of a lake.
Please bless me to remain within that state. ^3-33

<small>Spoken by the Tathagata Pamo Drupa.</small> ^3-34

Although completely pure like the limitless, empty sky,
Knowing each detail of Samsara and Nirvana exactly as they are;
You who manifest all kinds of activity for everyone,
Lord of Dharma, endowed with compassion and skill in means, ^3-35

to the precious Father and Sons of the Three Times and Timelessness,
with devotion of body, speech and mind, I prostrate at your lotus feet!
Completely clearing the darkness of my own and others’ deluded minds,
may we completely realize the rootless mind in itself! ^3-36

Dear Mother of great nonconceptual compassion,
to myself and all beings, all of your children,
to the supreme place of perfect stainless great bliss,
Please guide us with every kind of skillful means! ^3-37

By requesting you to fulfill your ancient commitments
Dharma Lord, Tathagata of the Three Times,
Please fulfill all of mine and others’ devoted wishes, and
Grant that supreme realization will quickly arise! ^3-38

The Dharma Lord’s commands were taught by the Great Sage.
Compassion and Great Bliss that never changes!
Please think of all of us, and grant your blessing, so that this delusion
swiftly becomes like that! ^3-39

All of my own and all other limitless beings’
accumulated karma and sudden conditions
Just like a mirror free of any rust
Please completely purify everything without exception! ^3-40

All the clouds of delusion, malice and conflict,
Cleared away by the strong wind of Original Awareness,
just like the vast mandala of the sun in the sky,
Please help us to quickly accomplish virtuous thoughts! ^3-41

By the virtue of respectfully making this prayer,
My I and all others attain the Dharmakaya in this life,
May the assembly of the Sangha always be harmonious, and
may the perfect Sangha accomplish vast benefit! ^3-42

<small>This also was spoken by Sangye Yarjon.</small> ^3-43

Hero who vanquished more than four foreign enemies,
Ishvara and the others, endowed with the sixfold omniscience and love,
displaying supreme qualities, adorned with all the marks and signs,
I prostrate to the Fathers and Sons who show the Three Criteria as one! ^3-44

Unmoving like the supremely solid Mount Sumeru,
Universal Emperor, without any equal,
effortlessly benefitting others, leading all to liberation,
Father and Son Sugatas of the Three Times, I prostrate to you! ^3-45

Three Lords, unconceived and inconceivable,
with the quality of limitless activity free from intention shaking the Three Worlds and establishing them in the
Supreme Realm
Guiding Dharma Lord Father and Sons, I prostrate to you! ^3-46

By the essence of the Sugatas of the Three Times, the Dharma Lord’s power,
Completely pacifying all outer and inner obscurations, harm, and hostility,
Endowed with mutually loving minds, attaining great bliss,
Please accomplish our wishes and cause the Dharma to spread and flourish! ^3-47

By this and all that I and others have accumulated in the Three Times,
In the eastern direction, the blissful immaculate Dharmata,
Loving King of Stainless Intelligence,
At the feet of Father and Son, Compassion itself, ^3-48

Myself and all others attaining unsurpassable supreme Enlightenment,
By the effortless Activity of the Single Character,
May the gathered disciples, as many as there may be,
be led to the state of the unsurpassed Dharma Lord! ^3-49

<small>Precious Sangye Yarjon spoke this prayer called “Turning Back the Mongols.”</small> ^3-50

Avalokitesvara from this special Northern Lineage,
Supreme holder of the Victor’s Doctrine of the rules of pure conduct,
May the Sun of the Glorious Great Taklung Tangpa’s
Accomplishment Lineage pervade Jambudvipa! ^3-51

May the gathering increase greatly in unshakeable joy
Without even hearing the names of illness, evil, or obstacles,
May completely pure good fortune fall like rain
And bless that the Doctrine’s Guardians and Protectors increase! ^3-52

By the blessing of the Kagyu Gurus, and
the truth of the Yidams’ accomplishments, and
by the power of the Dharma Protectors and Guards,
may the doctrine of the Accomplishment Lineage grow and flourish! ^3-53

### Indivisible Sixth Buddha Family ^3-2-0

Indivisible Sixth Buddha,Vajradhara,
Holder of the Six Oral Lineages, Telopa
Supreme accomplishment through austerity, Naropa,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-54

Supreme Nirmanakaya, known as Marpa the Translator,
Lord of Yogins, Milarepa
The one prophesied by the Buddha, Gampopa
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-55

Lord of the wise, Phamo Drupa
Victory banner of the Dharma, Taklungpa
Self-born Buddha known as Ratna,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-56

Great compassionate Sherab Drimay
One who frees all the intentions of the Tantras, Kirtishri,
Undeluded through abiding in Wisdom, Orgyen Gon,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-57

Lord of the Doctrine of the Accomplishment Lineage, known as Jina,
Holder of the treasure of nonconceptual compassion, known as Ratna,
One who realized the essential Definitive Meaning, known as Kirti,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-58

Lord of Supreme Wisdom, Protector Akshobhya,
Effortless Activity, known as Gonpo
Emanation of all the Victors, Lord of the World,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-59

Nirmanakaya with no equal, known as Pema
Powerful Vajra Holder, Saroruha-pada,
Learned, accomplished Kirti-pada,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-60

Venerable Ngawang Tashi Paldrup,
Lord of Dharma DrakpaLekdrup
Supreme Wisdom Manjushrisagara-pada
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-61

Clarifier of the Doctrine, known as Kirti,
Lord of the Family, Venerable Tendzin Namgyal,
Glorious Drakpa Rinchen Sherab,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-62

Venerable Tashi Drakpa Gyaltsen,
Lord of Refuge Rinchen Lekdrup, and
the one known as Kundu Choying Lhundrup,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-63

Venerable Tashi Chokyi Gyaltsen,
the one known as the Supreme Nirmanakaya Rinchen O Bar,
venerable Ngawang DrakpaKunsal,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-64

Trinlay Jampa Jungne, and
glorious Kunga Nampar Gyalwa,
incomparable Venerable Ngawang Lekdrup,
I pray to all the great masters of the past! ^3-65

One who manifests the most sacred Bodhicitta
as an uninterrupted flow of dramatic display,
precious Root Guru,
I pray to all the Kagyu Gurus! ^3-66

By this aspiration prayer of universal excellent activity,
appearing right now as the supreme deity,
glorious blood-drinking Heruka,
I pray to the assembly of Yidams! ^3-67

Transformed from grass and wood,
mother of all the Victors of the Three Times,
all samaya holders who work to protect,
I prostrate to the assembly of Dakinis! ^3-68

You who made a commitment in the presence of the Buddha,
by the dragon’s roar of Hung and Phat,
destroyer of great heaps of obstructing Maras,
I prostrate to all the Dharmapalas! ^3-69

To accomplish Buddhahood in one lifetime,
with a means of living compatible with Dharma,
you provide a treasure of all accumulations without exception,
I prostrate to all the Wealth Deities! ^3-70

I pray to the Guru, the precious Dharmakaya!
I pray to the compassionate Root Guru!
I pray to the lineage of accomplished Gurus!
I pray to the precious Gurus of the Command Lineage! ^3-71

I pray to the Guru, the precious Buddha of the three times! ^3-72

I pray to all the Gurus, Yidams, Dakinis, Dharmapalas and the assembly of Guardians! ^3-73

I pray for blessings so that the minds of myself and all sentient beings may follow the Dharma! ^3-74

I pray for blessings so that our Dharma may follow the Dharma!
I pray for blessings so that our Dharma may follow the Path!
I pray for blessings so that the Path may clarify delusion!
I pray for blessings so that delusion may shine forth as Original Awareness! ^3-75

I pray for blessings to remember impermanence and death!
I pray for blessings to be aware of the faults of Samsara!
I pray for blessings for the diligence to attain enlightenment in one lifetime!
I pray for blessings so that Samsara and Nirvana may be liberated in its own ground! ^3-76

I pray for blessings for the attainment of the supreme real accomplishment of Mahamudra! ^3-77

You embody all the completely pure omniscience and love of all the Victors combined,
holder of the victory banner of resounding auspicious benevolence,
glorious protector of all Samsara and Nirvana in this fortunate age,
I prostrate to you, wonderful supreme Nirmanakaya! ^3-78

From former times, by the vast mountain of the north,
in the Kalapa palace in the land of Shambhala,
in the beautiful grove of Malaya,
I prostrate to the supreme Rigden King! ^3-79

Then, in this valley surrounded by snow mountains,
bearing the name of Marton Rinchen Shakya,
authentic disciple of the powerful Shawaripa
I prostrate to you, who benefits all beings as they desire! ^3-80

Protector of Thatness, who now appears in this pure realm,
supreme siddha of the famous Wolf Valley,
one hundred year old Vidyadhara, ShonnuLodro
I prostrate to you, learned and accomplished universal crown ornament! ^3-81

Just like the lake born Padma,
naked of any stains to quality or realization,
actual unchanging vajra of supreme body, speech and mind,
I prostrate to the Guru, our protector until the end of existence! ^3-82

In that way, the garland of all the supreme births,
understanding everything, then arising to benefit others,
powerful Vajradhara, lord of the Four Kayas,
I prostrate to the Guru, the embodiment of all the Victors! ^3-83

By my praying and prostrating to you with reverence,
May I and other disciples, and all pure beings,
by the protector of the Three Realms, Namgyal Tashi,
be held close for all future lives! ^3-84

And for now, may whomever is endowed with the qualities of the higher realms
be completely victorious over all intervening obstacles,
and abiding within the view and conduct of noble joy,
may we perfectly accomplish the benefits of self and other! ^3-85

<small>This was written by the Lord’s own experienced disciple, Ngawang Namgyal, then clarified by the noble Jetsun Drolchok based on his example.</small> ^3-86

Glorious Victors of the Three Times, without exception,
gathering the ongoing cycle of the five inexhaustible adornments of being,
like springtime, perfectly clarifying beings and the teachings,
I pray to venerable Namgyal Palzang! ^3-87

Unchanging Vajra Body, Vairocana,
Brahma’s voice of Vajra Speech, Amitabha,
Mind Vajra of Great Bliss, Victor Akshobhya,
I pray to the supreme, all-pervading Lord of the Hundred Families! ^3-88

Distilled essence of the teachings of all the limitless Victors,
By the supreme path of the Mahamudra of reverent devotion,
sealing all the mandalas of knowledge,
I pray that all acceptance and rejection may arise as inseparable! ^3-89

May all desired things and their opposites, and so on, all transform
and by everything manifesting as Bodhicitta,
beginning with this pure, vast, aspiration prayer,
I pray that the Two Benefits will be effortlessly accomplished! ^3-90

To the glorious Guru, the embodiment of all Victors,
by my praying devotedly with one pointed mind,
may all beings, without exception, who fill all of space,
Accomplish the Guru Vajradhara! ^3-91

From this life onward, as long as enlightenment has not been attained,
From the Supreme Master who shows the essence of the holy Dharma,
the Glorious Guru himself, never being separate,
may I become a vessel for all the vast, profound Dharma! ^3-92

Through stable reverence, devotion, trust, and certainty,
through accomplishing the three ways of pleasing the master,
may all the secret key points of body, speech and mind
Be blended inseparably with my mind! ^3-93

By the invulnerable armour of Samsara and Nirvana,
with the single eye of the teachings of the precious Dharma,
by showing various forms to tame whomever is to be trained,
May all beings who pervade space attain complete enlightenment! ^3-94

May the intentions of the glorious Guru be fulfilled,
and may happiness arise for all beings that equal space,
and myself and every other being purifying obscurations and gathering accumulations,
Grant the blessing of swiftly attaining Buddhahood! ^3-95

<small>This is Bhikshu Ngawang Namgyal’s supplication to his Guru, the great Vajra Master Namgyal Rinchen Pal Zangpo.</small> ^3-96

Very embodiment of the omniscience and love of all Victors,
Like a god, manifesting Nirmanakaya for the benefit of beings,
Glorious Taklungpa, Gelek Jungnay,
I pray to Venerable Ngawang Namgyal! ^3-97

Lord of the World, known as the Incomparable Tangpa,
By manifesting everything as Dharmakaya,
multitude of appearances as the Garland of Golden Mountains of the Accomplishment Lineage,
I pray to the supreme all pervading Lord! ^3-98

As the Buddhas of uncountable previous ages
depending on immeasurable Great Compassion,
manifested in limitless realms for whomever was to be tamed,
I pray to the one who causes the Buddha’s Teachings to increase! ^3-99

Particularly, in this home of the Supreme Accomplishment Lineage,
By knowingly, fully, accepting the world,
with a mind that is the cause for many virtuous qualities arising,
I pray that you give rise to wonders everywhere! ^3-100

In this fortunate aeon, rich with the wealth of faith and wisdom,
From Vajradhara to the Venerable Masters of the past,
All you Lords of the Kagyu yourselves,
I pray you will manifest your promise! ^3-101

Through completely fulfilling this inexhaustible vow,
to guide all disciples gradually through the Dharma,
I pray that the ordained sangha be free of any impurities and defects,
and that they be endowed with stainless ethical conduct! ^3-102

Giving rise to the amazing, supreme Mind of Awakening,
gathering disciples by actualizing the unified Six Perfections,
establishing them in the higher states and Great Enlightenment,
I pray at the feet of the Great Bodhisattva! ^3-103

Supreme essence of an ocean of Vidyadharas and Scriptures,
through the unimpaired Kagyu blessing of Transference
Attained the highest realization of the profound Path,
I pray to the Supreme Lord of Siddhas! ^3-104

You, the fearless hero, victorious in all directions,
By your effortless, unceasing Enlightened Activity,
establishing all beings on the Bhumi of Great Bliss,
I pray at the feet of the Supreme Guide! ^3-105

For myself and all disciple beings without exception,
pacifying all situational and ultimate obstacles,
And for all wealth and glory, perfect and consistent with Dharma,
Please grant the blessing of them increasing more and more! ^3-106

<small>So, this was offered with devotion by the Buddhist monk Namgyal Rinchen Pal Zangpo at the Great Residence, when he closely, perfectly beheld the mandala of the face of the Supreme Venerable Nirmanakaya himself.</small> ^3-107

Treasure of an ocean of everything perfect,
Filled with the Dharma of the Essence of the Supreme Victor
Holder of the special, noble Complete Liberation, Taklungpa,
I prostrate to Ngawang Tashi Paldrup! ^3-108

Garland of emanations of ultimate blessing and realization,
Supreme Nirmanakaya of the Perfect Buddha,
single Original Awareness appearing in successive forms,
I prostrate to the one with the power of limitless compassion! ^3-109

Great Vidyadhara Sangye Yeshe, and
The one called Drington Shakya, who completed the Three Trainings,
The one endowed with the meaning of seeing and hearing, Orgyen Gonpo, and so on,
I prostrate to NamrolPakye Tenpa! ^3-110

So, the nondual Kaya of Mahamudra
One who knows all subjects, with powerful certainty,
Protector of the nine births of the Victory Banner of the Accomplishment Lineage Dharma
I prostrate to Master Tsemar Gyurpa! ^3-111

Then, as when at the top of a pure, green, wish-fulfilling tree,
an exquisite flower of the signs and examples shines,
that gives limitless joy and fulfilment with its quality and aroma,
I prostrate to the one who bestows the blessing of all desired accomplishments! ^3-112

Inexhaustible ornament of the inconceivable Three Secrets,
Effortless, Innate, Door of Liberation,
Visibly Manifest Nature, as has always been,
I prostrate to Choden YirabTrokpa! ^3-113

The wild geese of all sciences, without exception
Arise suddenly from desiring composite things
Inexhaustible intelligence that joins with whatever experience
I prostrate to the one with the power of vast, learned speech! ^3-114

You gathered the Two Accumulations with great power of meditation,
The Lord of the Family, supremely, effortlessly, and in a short time,
Bestowed profound and vast empowerments, authorizations, and key instructions
I prostrate to NodzayChokyi Gyamtso! ^3-115

Deeply immersed in the words and meaning of sutras, tantras, and commentaries,
thinking closely of his former studies and understandings,
having attained a never failing memory, his speech had no equal.
I prostrate to the Great Self-Arisen Omniscient One! ^3-116

Immeasurable self-arising courage,
Having heard extensively, his purity of heart increases,
Unity well attained, unobstructedly increasing
I prostrate to Nyen NgakPawoi Doda! ^3-117

Embodiment of the Realization of Venerable Sangye Yarjon
Supremely shining mirror of all conduct,
Abiding as he is, able to withstand analysis like gold,
I prostrate to the one who is wealthy in completely pure ethical conduct! ^3-118

You, the heir to all the Sages and their Sons,
Marvellous Lord of Bodhicitta,
By acting in great waves of the Bodhisattva’s conduct,
I prostrate to the only supreme guide of beings! ^3-119

EH, emptiness supremely endowed with all wisdom,
WAM, the supreme skillful means of Unchanging Great Bliss,
Their union is the Lord of all Buddha’s Vows,
I prostrate to the Great Vajra Master! ^3-120

Knowing that all Dharmas in the apparent world are desireless and unobstructed,
Pervading, permanent, non-referential compassion, without measure or clinging,
You, protector with the power to free the bonds of Samsara and Nirvana,
By the continually abiding, indestructible, essential bindu, ^3-121

May myself and others on the path of devotion, all disciples,
effortlessly accomplish all of our virtuous aims,
and may the Glorious Taklungpa’s famous
Dharma be a beautiful ornament for the world! ^3-122

<small>The peerless precious Lord of Dharma Ngawang Tashi Paldrup made this praise at the feet of the supreme NgedonChokyi Nyima Namdrol. Called the “Loud Drum of the Cloud of Faith,” this praise was requested by the Vajra Disciple DesheDamtsik’s patron, the wealthy Ngawang ChojinGyutso, and written by the Bhikshu Ngawang Namgyal in the eastern subtle channel, the supreme pure land of Vajra Mirror. Virtue and Happiness!</small> ^3-123

### The Prayer called “Precious Body” ^3-3-0

I pray to the venerable precious Body!
By the blessings of the precious Body,
may this, my ordinary body of ripened karma
attain the stainless Rainbow Body, I pray! ^3-124

I pray to venerable precious Speech!
By the blessings of precious Speech,
may all the stains of my meaningless speech be purified,
and may I attain the sixty qualities of the Voice of Brahma! ^3-125

I pray to the venerable precious Mind!
By the blessings of precious Mind,
may my mind stream, caught in the net of habitual tendencies,
attain the Mind of the Five Original Awarenesses! ^3-126

by the results of my accumulating merit,
by supremely serving the Guru with body and speech,
may those who accomplish with faith and reverence,
effortlessly attain experiences! ^3-127

The Guru’s blessing is the supreme cause
of the emanations of the Buddhas of the three times,
The disciple’s devotion is the supreme cause
of cutting the root of the foundation of samsara. ^3-128

Stopping the rain of material things is the cause
of developing revulsion for the appearances of attachment.
Beings like abandoned children are the cause
that increases the enlightened activity of loving mind. ^3-129

Venerable, incomparable Guru, Father and Sons,
and myself, remaining inseparable by my accomplishing whatever you command,
may mind’s delusions be purified, and
may I become a supreme guide for beings! ^3-130

At the time when I arise as an illusory body,
may the appearances of the Bardo not arise,
may the suffering of death shine forth as bliss,
and may I rest within the mind of luminosity! ^3-131

May the holy Dharma of the Vihara vastly expand!
May all limitless sentient beings be happy and blissful!
May the three bad states of Samsara become exhausted and empty!
May the classes of Maras with evil karma be pacified and tamed! ^3-132

May all Bodhisattvas, wherever they abide,
accomplish all of these aspiration prayers! ^3-133

<small>Spoken by the Sugata Taklungpa.</small> ^3-134

The “Namo Ratna Guru Dharmakaya”: ^3-135

All Great Beings, whomever they may be, wonderful and amazing,
with completely pure, stainless, luminous compassion,
completely abandoning all the obscurations of dualistic clinging,
I prostrate to You, Stainless Dharmakaya! ^3-136

All apparent phenomena in Samsara and Nirvana,
the indivisible, spontaneously accomplished natural Body,
unmoving from the equanimity of supreme Enlightenment,
I prostrate to You, nondual Great Bliss! ^3-137

without moving from supreme natural equanimity,
by the enlightened activity of Your Body, Speech and Mind,
You establish all beings in supreme enlightenment!
I prostrate to You, transcendent enlightened activity! ^3-138

By the precious refuge of the inseparable Three Kayas,
Body, Speech and Mind of the marvellous Victors and their heirs,
protector of beings who clears away all darkness,
I bow down to the Lord Regent’s supreme attainment! ^3-139

The biography of Your noble birth is extremely profound,
though without the acts that transcend the conceptual mind,
just the apparent taming the assembly of disciples,
I prostrate to your inconceivable biography! ^3-140

Your stainless ethics,
completely pure,
I prostrate to You,
completely pure ethics! ^3-141

All beings, without exception, who equal space,
by clothing them in the armor of ripening,
You establish all beings in supreme Enlightenment!
I prostrate to You, endowed with incomparable love! ^3-142

In all the great places, Orgyen and so on,
By pleasing all the assemblies of Viras and Yogins, and
all the assembled Dakinis, queens of the expanse,
I prostrate to You, stainless co-emergence! ^3-143

Completely abandoning all worldly elaborations,
With the activity of Rishis, abiding in the solitude of mountain hermitages
Banner of renown, famous throughout the Three Worlds,
I prostrate to the one who accomplishes all that is seen, heard, and intended! ^3-144

You, Supreme Person with an ocean of qualities,
for those like me, whose darkness is difficult to describe,
by the power of devotion, by even a little teaching,
unequalled great bliss, Lords of Dharma, Father and Sons ^3-145

until the ocean of samsara is emptied,
without being separated from you, even for an instant,
May all beings be established in supreme Enlightenment! ^3-146

To the Dharmakaya, Taklung Tangpa,
from JiktenGonpo of the Drigung,
I praise you with reverence!
May the Doctrine pervade all the great Bhumis! ^3-147

<small>This was written by DrigungKyobpa, and the last shloka was added as a praise by Venerable Ngawang Namgyal, incorporating some of Thangpa’s extensive biography in 17 chapters, and spoken based on this work.</small> ^3-148

Homage to Guru Limitless Jewels! ^3-149

Unequalled Dharma Lord, endowed with meaning and supreme signs,
king of wish-fulfilling power, who fulfills all needs, desires, and hopes,
glorious protector of existence and peace, meaningful to see,
I pray to you, deceased supreme being! ^3-150

Endowed with the sixty qualities of Brahma’s precious speech,
disciple’s Lord Protector, who shows the unmistaken path,
resounding with the holy dharma of the wonderful Supreme Vehicle,
I pray to you, deceased supreme being! ^3-151

Precious Mind, stainless and completely pure,
protector of beings, holding the treasure of supreme compassion,
continually beneficial, thinking only of the welfare of others,
I pray to you, deceased supreme being! ^3-152

source fulfilling all needs and desires, Lord of Dharma who cares for the Nagas,
wish-granting jewel who fulfills all disciples’ hopes and desires,
beyond expression, with qualities difficult to measure,
I pray to you, deceased supreme being! ^3-153

Conqueror of the four classes of Maras, endowed with the six powers,
Great compassion, accomplished conquering King of Sages,
effortless activity gloriously shining forth in Existence and Peace,
I pray to you, deceased supreme being! ^3-154

Supreme being, endowed with Body, Speech, Mind, and Qualities,
Vajradhara, indivisible perfect activity,
realizing the inconceivable Three Secrets,
supremely joyful, please hold me in your heart! ^3-155

by the virtue of supplicating and prostrating to you,
in that stainless radiant eastern pure land,
at the feet of the Guides, the Lords of Dharma, father and sons,
may I be born in the miraculous lotus throne. ^3-156

<small>So, this praise of the Late Supreme Being, Ratnanatha, spoken by the Venerable Sangye Wonpo Dorje, was clarified slightly by Taklungpa Ngawang Namgyal and, in arranging it, none of my own work was added.</small> ^3-157

Homage to the Precious Guru Hevajra! ^3-158

Glorious, indestructible, powerful vajra body,
precious refuge, faultless and completely pure,
by the light rays of original awareness, meaningful to behold,
and clearing away all ignorance, I praise Lord Yarjon! ^3-159

Foundation of unobstructed gloriously arising qualities,
song of the great resounding emptiness of stainless supreme illumination,
Mother who has attained the result of the supreme speech of the Mahayana,
I praise you, Lord of Dharma who destroys the Four Maras! ^3-160

Mind realizing the omniscience of nonconceptual luminosity,
unchanging hero, treasury of all Victorious Ones,
expanse of Mahamudra, the nonduality of Samsara and Nirvana,
I praise you, completely enlightened Lord of Dharma! ^3-161

Vajradhara, accomplished conqueror, compassionate ruler,
who skillfully and lovingly guides all sentient beings,
Precious Guru, wish-fulfilling treasure,
I praise you, Lord of Dharma, the glory of Samsara and Nirvana! ^3-162

Accomplishing all wishes and aims just as desired,
expanse of the inseparable, nondual, self-liberated mind,
indefinite, unmeasurable Thatness
I bow at the feet of the Dharma Lord, Great Bliss of the Expanse of Awareness! ^3-163

By the blessings of reverence for You, precious one,
May I and all sentient beings who equal space,
Purifying all obscurations of body, speech and mind,
realize qualities equal to the Threefold Lord! ^3-164

<small>This, written by the essence of all Buddhas, Precious Guru Sangye Yarjon, who was the nephew of Sangye Won, his mind filled with supreme joy at meeting a yogin of Hevajra, Avadhutipa, a perfect Rishi, in the snake year, the monkey month, on the tenth day of the waxing moon, is completed.</small> ^3-165

All Tathagatas, illusory original awareness,
Magical fame, consistent with the Lord’s intention,
completely enjoying all the objects of thought,
I pray to you, glorious, famous, supreme emanation! ^3-166

By natural, effortless, purposeful action,
glorious, learned, venerable, excellent one who has left the top,
foundation of the perfect Buddha who has realized luminosity,
I pray to you, glorious, renowned supreme emanation! ^3-167

Instantly, all the environment and beings of Do Kham,
the actual immaculate teacher and students,
Amazing holy accomplishment, written by his hand,
I pray to you, glorious, renowned supreme emanation! ^3-168

By the aspiration prayer of the conduct of Samantabhadra,
King of all the beings of destructible Samsara,
Quickly accomplishing, speaking evenly until the end,
grant your blessing that all falsehoods may be stirred out from the depths! ^3-169

<small>All Blessings! Virtue! Virtue!</small> ^3-170

## 4. The Sutra Remembering the Three Jewels ^4-0

In the language of India: aryaratnatraya anu smriti sutra ^4-1

In the language of Tibet: ‘phags pa dkonmchoggsumrjessudranpa’imdo ^4-2

I prostrate to the Three Jewels! ^4-3

Thus, the Buddha, the accomplished transcendent conqueror, the tathagata who has attained thatness, the arhat who has conquered all foes, is a perfectly and completely enlightened buddha, ^4-4

endowed with awareness and worthy of reverence, ^4-5

the Sugata who has gone to bliss, ^4-6

knower of the entire world, ^4-7

guide and tamer of beings, ^4-8

unsurpassable, ^4-9

teacher of gods and humans, the Buddha, accomplished transcendent conqueror, ^4-10

that Tathagata is compatible with the causes of pure merit, ^4-11

he does not waste the sources of virtue, ^4-12

he is supremely adorned with patience, ^4-13

he is the foundation of all the treasures of merit, ^4-14

adorned with all the excellent minor marks, ^4-15

and the full flowering of the major marks, ^4-16

with timely and appropriate activity, ^4-17

with nothing displeasing to see, ^4-18

He brings complete joy to those who have faith and devotion, ^4-19

He has unconquerable wisdom, ^4-20

His powers are invincible, ^4-21

He is the teacher of all sentient beings, ^4-22

the father of all bodhisattvas, ^4-23

the king of all noble beings, ^4-24

the guide who leads all beings to the city of transcending sorrow, ^4-25

with measureless original awareness, ^4-26

with inconceivable fearlessness, ^4-27

with completely pure speech ^4-28

in melodious tones, ^4-29

one is never tired of seeing him, ^4-30

his embodied form is incomparable, ^4-31

unobscured by the realm of desire, ^4-32

unclothed by the realm of form, ^4-33

not adulterated with the formless realm, ^4-34

completely liberated from all suffering, ^4-35

totally liberated from all the aggregates, ^4-36

not encumbered by the elements, ^4-37

in complete control of the sense processes, ^4-38

He has completely severed all the knots, ^4-39

He is completely liberated from all agony, ^4-40

He is liberated from existence, ^4-41

he has crossed over the river, ^4-42

He has completely perfected original awareness ^4-43

(abiding in) all the past and future ^4-44

and present Buddha Bhagavan’s original awareness, ^4-45

not abiding in Nirvana, ^4-46

He abides at the limit of perfection itself, ^4-47

abiding on the level where all sentient beings are seen, ^4-48

all these are the authentic and supreme qualities of the embodied form of the Buddha Bhagavan. ^4-49

The holy Dharma is good at the beginning, good in the middle and good at the end, ^4-50

It is excellent in meaning, in words, and in syllables, ^4-51

It is unadulterated, utterly complete, absolutely pure, and absolutely purifying, ^4-52

it is taught perfectly by the Accomplished Transcendent Conqueror. ^4-53

it brings perfect vision, ^4-54

It is unafflicted, uninterrupted, trustworthy to apply, ^4-55

encountering it fulfills all aspirations, ^4-56

It is validated through the individual experience of the wise, ^4-57

the Dharma taught by the Bhagavan consists entirely on taming the mind, ^4-58

it is the arising of certainty, ^4-59

causing one to arrive at complete enlightenment. ^4-60

It is without contradiction, including everything, ^4-61

stable and unmoving, ^4-62

as for the Mahayana Sangha, they abide excellently, ^4-63

they abide with awareness, abide with honesty, abide in harmony, ^4-64

They are worthy of veneration with folded hands, ^4-65

worthy of veneration by prostration, ^4-66

they are a glorious field of merit, ^4-67

offering to them brings great purification, ^4-68

they are an object for generosity, ^4-69

they are absolutely the greatest object for generosity. ^4-70

The Buddha’s qualities are inconceivable.
The Dharma’s qualities are inconceivable.
The Noble Sangha is inconceivable.
By having confidence in the inconceivable, ^4-71

complete liberation is also inconceivable!
May we be born in the completely pure realm! ^4-72

<small>The Sutra of Remembering the Noble Three Jewels is complete.</small> ^4-73

## 5. The Third Event of the Day (Sojong Vow) ^5-0

The Guide, the Accomplished Transcendent Conqueror, Tathagata, Arhat, perfectly complete Buddha, endowed with perfect knowledge and perfect conduct, ^5-1

Sugata, Knower of the World, ^5-2

piloting the ship that tames beings, ^5-3

unsurpassable guide of all gods and humans, ^5-4

I prostrate to the Buddha, the Accomplished Transcendent Conqueror, Glorious Victor Shakyamuni ^5-5

I make offerings, I go for refuge! ^5-6

### Praise and Prostration ^5-1-0

When the supreme one among humans was born,
He took seven steps on this great ground,
and said “I am supreme in this world.”
I prostrated to you, wise at that time! ^5-7

Your completely pure, mighty, supremely excellent body,
is like an ocean of wisdom, like a heap of gold,
whose fame radiates throughout the Three Worlds,
I prostrate to you, supremely accomplished Protector! ^5-8

Face like a stainless moon, endowed with the supreme signs,
I prostrate to you, who shines like gold!
Immaculate one, with no peer in the three realms of existence,
I prostrate to you, an incomparable omniscient one! ^5-9

Protector, endowed with great compassion,
who guides by omniscience,
Oceanic field of merit and qualities,
I prostrate to you, Tathagata! ^5-10

Free from all desires through purity,
with virtue that liberates from the bad states,
single source of supreme ultimate meaning,
I prostrate to the Dharma of Peace! ^5-11

Liberated, then showing the path to liberation,
training by abiding in supreme purity,
possessing all the qualities of the pure realms,
I also prostrate to the Sangha! ^5-12

### Sutra ^5-2-0

Do not commit a single degrading action!
Accomplish virtue perfectly!
Completely tame your own mind!
This is the doctrine of the Buddha. ^5-13

Like stars, cataracts, butter lamp flames,
illusions, dew, water bubbles, and-
dreams, lightning, and clouds,
View all composite dharmas in thar way. ^5-14

### Dedication ^5-3-0

By this merit, may I become omniscient,
and having attained it, eliminating all the enemies of faults,
may all beings be liberated from the stormy waves of birth, old age, sickness and death
of the ocean of existence. ^5-15

## 6. The Praise of the Twelve Deeds of the Bhagavan, the Powerful Sage ^6-0

Will skillful means and compassion, you took birth among the Shakyas,
You destroyed the forces of Mara, undefeatable by others.
Your body shines like a golden Mount Sumeru,
I prostrate to you, King of the Shakyas! ^6-1

You first gave birth to the Mind of Awakening,
and completing the accumulations of merit and awareness,
your deeds in this age are vast!
Protector, I praise you! ^6-2

You benefitted the gods, then knowing it was time to tame beings,
You descended from the god’s realm like an elephant,
You saw the caste of a magical emanation of a goddess,
and entered her womb, I prostrate to this deed! ^6-3

Completing ten months, this son of the Shakyas
was born in the auspicious grove of Lumbini,
Brahma and Indra praised your supreme signs
of the enlightened family, I prostrate to this deed! ^6-4

Youth of great strength, a lion of all men,
You demonstrated your great power at Anghamagada,
and destroying all those great arrogant ones,
You who are without peer, I prostrate to this deed! ^6-5

In order to act in harmony with worldly dharmas,
and avoid unseemliness, you took a queen
and retinue, and with skillful means,
You protected the kingdom; I prostrate to this deed! ^6-6

Seeing that worldly affairs have no essence,
renouncing your household, you departed into space.
By the Stupa of Complete Purity, you yourself
ordained yourself, I prostrate to this deed! ^6-7

With the intention to accomplish Bodhi through effort,
by the River Nairanjnana, for six years,
practicing austerities to the limit of diligence,
and attained supreme Dhyana, I prostrate to this deed! ^6-8

Since your efforts from beginningless time were meaningful,
in Magadha, under the Bodhi Tree,
in unmovable vajra posture, attaining complete Buddhahood,
and perfect Enlightenment, I prostrate to that deed! ^6-9

Then, looking quickly at all beings with compassion,
in Varanasi and other supreme places,
You turned the wheel of Dharma, and all disciples
were established in the Three Yanas, I prostrate to that deed! ^6-10

Destroying the malicious objections of others,
the six Tirthaka teachers, Devadatta and others,
You subdued all the Maras in the land of Varanasi,
Sage victorious in battle, I prostrate to that deed! ^6-11

Your qualities are unequalled in the three realms of existence,
You showed wonderful miracles in Shravasti,
all gods and humans make supreme offerings to you,
You who cause the Doctrine to flourish, I prostrate to that deed! ^6-12

To quickly encourage all those who are lazy,
on the pure ground of Kushinagara,
You departed the deathless vajra-like body
and transcended sorrow, I prostrate to that deed! ^6-13

Because in reality there is no destruction, and
so that beings may accumulate merit in the future,
in that place, you emanated many relics,
the eight parts of your remains, I prostrate to that deed! ^6-14

Like this, the Lord of the Dharma, accomplished transcendent conqueror,
By this small praise of the manner of these deeds,
and all beings conducting themselves with virtue,
may we equally accomplish these deeds of the Sugata! ^6-15

A body like yours, Tathagata, and
a retinue, life span, pure realm, and
supreme perfect signs just like yours,
may I and all others become exactly like that! ^6-16

By the power of praising and supplicating you,
May I and all others, wherever we may be, in any part of the world,
pacify all illness, poverty, fighting and quarrelling,
and experience the increase of Dharma and good fortune, we pray! ^6-17

The teacher appearing in the world, and
the Dharma that illuminates like sunlight, and
the teaching and accomplishment of Dharma-holding teachers and students increasing,
may there be the good fortune of the Dharma remaining for a long time! ^6-18

## 7. Praise of the Sage ^7-0

Homage to Shakyamuni! ^7-1

Because He is the glory of the world and nirvana,
Pinnacle of the Shakyas, happily accomplished;
To that guide for all beings of the three realms,
I prostrate with supreme reverence! ^7-2

On the page of the joyful Kshatriya caste,
brought forth with the writing brush of merit
the excellently drawn pictures on his soles,
accomplishing benefit and happiness through the appearance of pure qualities, ^7-3

dependent on limitless compassion,
Your Body powerful like the king of elephants,
naturally uncontaminated like the great ocean,
being supported for a full ten months, ^7-4

effortlessly, from his mother’s hip,
He moved and naturally came forth
Beautiful, under a wish-fulfilling tree,
a tree completely rich with signs and examples, ^7-5

a field of knowledge and the arts,
clearer and brighter than the light-giving sun,
for those malicious adversaries who are completely corrupted,
evil spirit birds and malicious spirits, ^7-6

with all their sixty thousand alluring wives,
even offering the five sensual pleasures,
like a lotus blooming in the wilderness of a foul swamp,
never clothed by conditioned existence, ^7-7

having crossed the river of the Victors’ children,
departing to the completely pure stupa,
Your Body is the golden Mount Sumeru,
Your upper robe, the colour of the sunrise, embraces you like a maiden, ^7-8

Brahmin Rishis, without exception
unable to follow his unique example,
by the power of austerities, difficult to bear,
attaining the bliss of twenty-four factors, ^7-9

at the summit of the Vajra Seat,
on a mat of blue-green grass,
supremely abiding in the unmovable vajra posture,
By his power, like ten million hurricanes, ^7-10

unwilling Mara’s deluded mind,
all the many distorted manifestations
of Mara’s desires
are destroyed by the samadhi of love. ^7-11

Skillfully piloting the ship of meditation
on the great ocean of samsara, difficult to measure,
although he can cross over in an instant,
He has become the guru of the three worlds, ^7-12

With the wheel of the One Born from a Golden Egg (Brahma).
By praying with devotion in Your presence,
with five Bhikshus in the centre of a circle of Devas,
You beat the royal drum of the Dharma of the Four Truths, ^7-13

for Sattvas endowed with tenfold power, and
Bull of Speech, with an assembly of Sravakas,
who offered a fertile field of devotion and virtue,
with a rainfall of the Dharma of selflessness, ^7-14

staying happily in the beautiful house of the Three Yanas,
all those who abide will be henceforth taken under care,
and finally, opening everything excellently,
You supremely turned the Wheel of Dharma. ^7-15

The lotus garden of disciples, without exception,
are all protected by the sunlight of compassion. Then,
between two Sal trees,
You manifested passing into Nirvana. ^7-16

Your incomparable relics,
like the fabulous royal wish-granting gem,
become a lamp for the merit of this destructible world of gods
and human beings. ^7-17

Finally, as long as the world exists,
by manifesting a display of emanations,
and ripening innumerable disciples,
I continually bow down to your enlightened activity! ^7-18

By the power of praying in this way,
reaching the river crossing of your teachings,
May I, along with all who become your disciples,
attain the state of omniscience! ^7-19

<small>So, in order to benefit and bring joy to sincere students, the all-knowing Lord of Speech spoke this on the Holy Day of Sravana, on the twelfth day of the waxing moon, and written down by Ngawang Namgyal at the Great Vihara of the Glorious Taklung. Sarva Mangalam!</small> ^7-20

## 8. Praise of the King of Sages and Disciples Called The Loud Sound of Drums That Awakens All Beings Through Great Equanimity ^8-0

Ocean of Jewels’ generosity, the Two Accumulations
gathered and completely polished in the Dharmata,
by the Great Truth, inconceivable and completely pure,
placed as ornaments of the pure realms of the ten directions, all ^8-1

excellence subsumed into one, then descended here!
Peerless Lord of Dharma, Great Sage,
with your retinue of the assembly of noble Arhats,
arriving through the power of your heart vow, please abide here! ^8-2

Through the power of your loving aspirations, great, vast, and profound,
Gloriously arriving at the pinnacle of all the Victorious Ones,
Lord who turns the wheel of the Dharma of the Three Yanas
I bow down to the Entity of all Perfect Buddhas! ^8-3

Shiva on Mt Kailash, form of virtuous merit,
source of an ocean of the supports for benefit and happiness,
drum that dispels longing, holder of incense with pervading perfume,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand three hundred! ^8-4

Radiant glittering crystal cave of the Dharma of Accomplishment,
unconquered by the objects of evil Maras,
They have been completely assimilated and bound.
I bow down to Arhat with one hundred disciples! ^8-5

In the dense forest of trees with aromatic leaves
The supreme noble Arhat Vanavasin, who has exhausted all contamination,
His peaceful scorpion-finger mudra a wish fulfilling tree of jewels,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand four hundred! ^8-6

In the precious, dreamlike realm of Zang Ling,
while taming disciples as a loving friend,
holding beautiful golden earrings,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-7

Indra’s presence, naturally arrived at the lion throne
The great Arhat Vajriputra
Chamaradhara, who gives a cooling threatening mudra,
I bow down to the Arhat with a thousand retinue! ^8-8

By the gently flowing waters of the Yamuna River,
completely liberated from existence, perfectly endowed,
teaching the Dharma and remaining in equanimity, overcoming evil by a mere sight,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-9

In Kashmir, in the grove of the perfect Gesar,
Kanakavatsa, object of worship for the lineage of Nagas,
binding them with the precious lasso of compassionate words and meaning,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-10

With an excellent tall form on the island of Godaniya,
Supreme Kanaka, with most excellent wisdom from mindfulness,
sealed by resting evenly in samadhi,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-11

Quickly accomplishing your aspiration in the northern continent, Uttarakuru,
in the centre, the Lord of Dharma Speech, Bakula
patiently bestowing many different jewels, holding a mongoose,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-12

Meeting and expanding bliss in the island of perfumed cherries,
Rahula, with supreme reverence for the teachings of the Victors and Sons,
wearing the helmet of countless precious actions,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-13

At the royal palace of the renowned Vulture Peak Mountain,
Ksudrapanthaka, who supremely and completely pacified the afflictions,
in great stable awareness, holding the mudra of equanimity,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-14

Living five hundred lifetimes on the especially exalted island,
Pindola Bharadvaja, free of all evil,
reading a volume of the Buddha’s Word, holding a begging bowl,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand esteemed ones! ^8-15

In the Realm of the Three, on Indra’s crown ornament,
the Honored One, mantrinPanthaka,
reading a volume of the Pitaka of the Supreme Vehicle,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-16

Teaching about the breadth of all glittering manifestation,
Protecting from the abyss of samsara and nirvana, Nagasena,
holding a cooling, excellent vase of treasure,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-17

Expounding Vihula on the beautiful ornament of Mt Meru
the noble Shravaka Gopaka,
understanding the book of the Buddha’s teachings concerning time
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-18

By the banks of the Sindh River, on the peak of the snow mountains,
with the unchanging wealth of intelligence and liberation,
holding a Dharmakaya Maha Bodhi Stupa,
I bow down to the Arhat with one thousand two hundred! ^8-19

Spoken words that pacify and free from the darkness of conditioned existence,
Clouds of afflictions are scattered by the antidote of the wind
thousand lights of understanding exhaustion and non-arising,
I bow down to the Sun of happiness and benefit and his retinue! ^8-20

Older brother, heart son of all Sages without exception,
Lord Avalokiteshvara, who holds the seal of Amitabha,
In the manner of an Upasaka, holding a tail-fan and anointing vase,
I bow down to the great attendant of all Noble Ones! ^8-21

With joy in offering with reverent faculties,
in the presence of the Victor along with his disciples,
because limited offerings spread on a table were not possible,
I bow down to the Patron, the Great Ha Shang! ^8-22

In the east, Dhritarashtra guards by strength,
in the south, Virudhaka, born of the noble Mahayana family,
In the west, Virupaksha, wrathful at the enemies of the Dharma,
In the north, Vaishravana, who bestows everything desired, ^8-23

In that way, the mandala of purity alone
the support of those treasures of compassion, realization and omniscience,
by mindful awareness, stable motivation is unmistaken
with the clarity of understanding, I bow down! ^8-24

I make actually existing and mentally emanated offerings!
I lay aside all hindrances to the higher realms and liberation!
I rejoice in the great waves of vast Buddha Activity!
I pray that you guard the holy Dharma with no hesitation! ^8-25

Please benefit living beings today and for a long time!
By the virtue of the Dharma of Scripture and Realization,
So that disciples’ minds may grow and flourish forever,
Grant your blessings that all will be accomplished as we wish! ^8-26

<small>So, at the great seat of the Glorious Taklung, the Sangha were ordained as full Bhikshus, which they perfectly accepted over a half-month’s time. When taking the mending-purification vows, after reciting the
Pratimoksha Sutra, they requested this prayer, and the Bhikshu Ngawang Namgyal composed this with delight in the immaculate Upper Retreat. Virtue!</small> ^8-27

## 9. The Tea Offering: Deathless Amrita ^9-0

Protector of samsara and nirvana, peerless embodiment of all refuges,
precious treasury of supreme and ordinary siddhis,
to my compassionate father root guru,
I offer this tea of deathless amrita! ^9-1

Protector of beings, glorious Khyentse Wangchuk,
Dharma Lord, Venerable Taklung Tangpa,
to you with your precious lineage,
I offer this tea of deathless amrita! ^9-2

In the countless mandalas of deities of the tantras,
Lord of the Sages, taming all who are to be tamed in every realm,
to all your heirs and all their disciples,
I offer this tea of deathless amrita! ^9-3

Long ago, you were appointed to protect the Dharma
Glorious Awareness Protector, together with your consort,
With the divine UpasakaYigyal (Lynx King) and his retinue,
I offer this tea of deathless amrita! ^9-4

In this life and in all of my future lives,
peerless holy protectors, from all of you,
may I never be separated even for an instant,
and may I realize the supreme Dharma of Mahamudra! ^9-5

Venerable Supreme Guide, Dharma King,
Your unchanging vajra body on a lion throne,
Your speech, the song of Brahma, like a rainfall of Dharma,
I pray you will protect your disciples with loving heart, ^9-6

establish our mother sentient beings in bliss, and
cause the doctrine of the Royal Sage to flourish, and
pacifying all opposing circumstances and obstacles,
may we quickly attain the supreme kingdom of Buddha! ^9-7

<small>So, after being requested by one steward who needed a tea offering prayer, I, Ngawang Namgyal Trashi, praised thus.</small> ^9-8

## 10. Prayer to the Guru and Lineage ^10-0

Whose kindness is Great Bliss itself,
instantly shining forth and filling the world,
Guru’s embodied form, like a jewel,
I bow down to your vajra lotus feet! ^10-1

Unchanging great bliss with illusory form,
unified with the Wisdom Consort,
glory of all conditioned existence and Nirvana,
I prostrate to powerful Vajradhara! ^10-2

You who, like extracting the oil from sesame,
Are great Original Awareness abiding in a body,
Principal Lord of Dakinis, excellently seeing,
I prostrate to Venerable Tilopa! ^10-3

Without being discouraged by all difficulties,
You, Learned One, in an instant
were completely liberated by the highest path,
I prostrate to Glorious Naropa! ^10-4

Supremely abiding in the ocean of the Noble Land,
Mahasiddha who relied with devotion on the King of Nagas (Buddha),
Possessing the jewel treasury of the Hearing Lineage,
I prostrate to Marpa the Translator! ^10-5

By the strong earthquake of diligent accomplishment,
obtaining supreme complete realization,
Master of all the Lords of Accomplished Ones,
I prostrate to Laughing Vajra (Milarepa)! ^10-6

Holder of the prophecy of the Lion of the Shakyas,
By disciplined hearing and thinking,
holding the victory banner of the teachings of the Practice Lineage,
I prostrate to Youthful Moonlight (Gampopa)! ^10-7

Entering the heart of reasoning and scriptural authority,
completely liberated by supreme realization,
Holding all of the Guide’s Holy Dharma,
I prostrate to Dorje Gyalpo (Phagmodrupa) ! ^10-8

Supremely ornamented by accumulated ethical conduct,
Without any rivals or equals
in attaining the mastery of samadhi,
I prostrate to Taklung Tangpa! ^10-9

Combining the rivers of the Kadampa and Mahamudra,
perfectly holding the tradition of the Charioteers,
You became the protector of all beings,
I prostrate to Ratnanatha! ^10-10

Completely liberated from all faults,
perfectly adorned with the ornaments of ethical conduct,
Holding the victory banner of unending accomplishment,
I prostrate to the Wisdom Guru! ^10-11

Fully manifest omniscient Original Awareness,
You became a treasure of nonconceptual love
through an unending flow of enlightened activity,
I prostrate to the auspicious Guru! ^10-12

Learned in all objects of knowledge,
supremely accomplishing complete liberation,
clarifying the doctrines of scripture and realization,
I prostrate to Sangye Palzang! ^10-13

Purifying all the latent tendencies of the two obscurations,
treasure of all the qualities of realization,
limitless power and enlightened activity,
I prostrate to Ratna Guru! ^10-14

Protector, the excellent vase of your heart,
filled with the purifying water of omniscient love,
ripening and liberating all beings,
I prostrate to Ratnakara! ^10-15

In the traditions of the authoritative teachings of sutra and tantra,
Perfectly completing all hearing and thinking,
diligently accomplishing his own original state,
I prostrate to the Great One who has renounced everything! ^10-16

Essence of all the Buddhas of the three times,
Treasury of the 84,000 Dharmas,
Leader of the ocean of the supreme noble Sangha,
I prostrate to the mass of auspicious glory! ^10-17

You are the unity of all acts of omniscient love
by all Buddhas without exception,
the only friend of all living beings,
I prostrate to the one with the signs of enlightenment! ^10-18

Steadfast in all the roots of merit,
You touch the sky at the peak of Original Awareness,
Wish-fulfilling tree, rainfall of all things desired,
I prostrate to the light of auspicious glory! ^10-19

Whatever excellent teachings there may be
are perfectly summarized by his discerning intelligence,
the stainless teachings are made more excellent.
I prostrate to the Lord of Speech! ^10-20

Making all auspiciousness more auspicious,
Excellently ornamented by all glory,
To all those of great renown,
I praise you with supreme reverence! ^10-21

The light rays of your embodied form
are supremely victorious over all conflicting conditions,
and renowned for the assembly of disciples’ supportive conditions
I prostrate to the guiding Lord of Dharma! ^10-22

In the centre of the vast ocean of merit
filled with the glorious jewels of the Kagyu,
move the successive waves of perfect Activity,
I prostrate to the Guru of living beings! ^10-23

By the compassionate power of the completely omniscient Victors,
Wonderful auspicious Nirmanakaya
holding the victory banner of ancient renown,
I prostrate to the perfect glorious Protector! ^10-24

Treasure of many Victors’ omniscient love,
by the celebration of perfect excellent teachings,
supremely bestowing ripening and liberation on those to be tamed,
I prostrate to the Holy Master! ^10-25

Glorious joyful bliss of any and all beings,
skillfully sending a rainfall of auspicious Dharma,
You cause the sprouts of complete liberation to grow,
I prostrate to the great heroic being! ^10-26

Pervading Lord, vajra body of great bliss,
unconquerable glory of all conditioned existence and peace,
with eternal and permanent Buddha Activity,
I prostrate to Ngawang Namgyal! ^10-27

Treasury of the ocean of eloquent supreme teachings,
glorious doctrine of the auspicious Practice Lineage,
with wonderful spontaneously accomplished activity,
I prostrate to Choktrul Dampa! ^10-28

Gloriously filling the ocean of the Two Accumulations,
perfect accumulation of the ethics of Original Awareness,
Only protector of all beings, including the gods,
I prostrate to DrakpaLekdrup! ^10-29

Completely destroying great heaps of the class of Maras,
Pacifying all afflicted concepts, without exception,
Completely filling the mandala of the wise,
I prostrate to Jampal Gyatso! ^10-30

Natural embodiment of all Victors’ compassion,
Supreme banner of both temporal and spiritual traditions,
Directly lifting up this conditioned world,
I prostrate to Ngawang Drakpa! ^10-31

Filling the minds of both sutra and mantra,
treasure of many glorious qualities,
you bestow all ordinary and supreme accomplishments
I prostrate to Trinlay Tendzin! ^10-32

Holder of the white lotus, protector of beings,
who is like the Second Buddha
Great body, the only glory of benefit and happiness,
I prostrate to Drakpa Rinchen! ^10-33

From the ocean of previous training in the two accumulations,
By the wish-fulfilling jewel of great love,
You clear away all the poverty of Samsara and Nirvana
I prostrate to NyendrakPalzang! ^10-34

You perceive with no delusion the natural state as it is,
Knowing all objects of knowledge as they appear,
Naturally benefitting self and others,
I prostrate to Rinchen Lekdrup! ^10-35

In the ocean of countless Victorious Ones,
You combine the three qualities of omniscience, love, and ability in one,
Incomparable spiritual friend of this Doctrine,
I prostrate to ChoyingLhundrup! ^10-36

Here and now, the essential teachings
among all beings of the ten directions with their families,
blaze with fame in the realm of ordinary beings,
I prostrate to Chokyi Paljor! ^10-37

Supreme victory banner of the doctrines of scripture and realization,
Is completely, supremely, and permanently raised
at the pinnacle of conditioned existence,
I prostrate to Drakpa Gyatso! ^10-38

All clouds of acts of omniscient,
powerful love of the Victors of the three times with their heirs,
Swirl like a cloak around the glory of your mind.
I prostrate to DrakpaKunsal! ^10-39

The hundred thousand suns of omniscient love
clear away the darkness of ignorance of the Three Worlds
With the glorious quality of naturally accomplishing the two benefits,
I prostrate to LodroDrimay! ^10-40

The three secrets of all Buddhas
are gathered into one in your own body itself.
Appearing in the form of Vajradhara,
I prostrate to KungayTsenchen! ^10-41

Treasury of an ocean of the qualities of renunciation and realization,
with Enlightened Activity for the benefit of others
Treasury of an ocean of Dharma traditions of scripture and realization,
I prostrate to Ngawang Lekdrup! ^10-42

May I, from this time forward,
Until the essence of Enlightenment is reached,
To the Guru, the embodiment of all Buddhas,
and all the assembled ones, I go for refuge! ^10-43

Clouds of outer, inner and secret offerings
My own body and also my enjoyments,
Whatever virtue I’ve accumulated in the three times,
I offer all of that to all of you! ^10-44

From beginningless Samsara,
under the power of ignorant disrespect and
all heedless emotional afflictions,
I free admit and lay aside all faults! ^10-45

The supreme Guru and Victors, and
the Victors’ heirs (Bodhisattvas) and Pratyekabuddhas,
Sravakas, and ordinary people,
I rejoice in all of their virtue! ^10-46

For those with lesser intelligence who have difficulty realizing,
who don’t understand the teachings of Dharma,
may all the Guides and myself,
turn the wheel of Dharma! ^10-47

For as long as Samsara exists,
not passing into Nirvana, but remaining forever,
Accomplishing benefit for myself and all others,
I pray to you with sincere intention! ^10-48

Just as all the Victors
and their heirs have completely dedicated
This virtue, and all other virtues,
I dedicate to the great enlightenment of all beings! ^10-49

<small>Thus, this prayer in verse form to all the Kagyu Rinpoches was written with a devoted mind by the BhikhshuNgaggi Wangpo. From Ngawang Namgyal on down, the lineage prayers were previously amended by others.</small> ^10-50

## 11. This is the Supplication of the Kagyu called the Manika Crystal ^11-0

<small>In the presence of Jedrung Rinpoche at the upper retreat of Rachung, on the occasion of consecrating a painting of the lineage masters, I heard you teach on the heritage of the deity. When I supplicated, Won Heruka with all his attributes appeared in the thangka, and I offered the seven branches and prayed. He was adorned with the attributes of ordination, with vajra and kartari in his left hands and his right holding a ghanta, and kapala filled with amrita. Then I requested the amrita from the great powerful Hevajra. When the great powerful Hevajra gave it to me, the swirling amrita that I was able to drink had an inexhaustible, extremely sweet flavour, and as I sipped by stages, I thought that this dharma was useless. I said I was unable to finish it, and as I said that, he snapped his fingers and said drink one more mouthful! Arising from the mandala, the Great Powerful One gave it to me, and the one called Lilavajra prayed: “Please bestow all your collected teachings on us!” “In order to bless the disciples, a ganachakra vast feast offering is necessary,” he said. Offering the ganachakra, he spoke these words: “Enjoy this inexhaustible ganachakra as great original awareness!” Then, we said, “A La La! Please give us a prayer to the Kagyu Rinpoches!” Then he spoke this prayer called the “Supplication of the Manika Crystal.”</small> ^11-1

In the space-like Dharmata,
Taking up the form of the Great Rupakaya,
Sambhogakaya supremely adorned with the Signs and Examples,
Sixth Buddha of the Family of Signs, Vajradhara,
I pray to that Guru!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-2

You took human birth in the Brahmin Caste,
holder of the six lineages of the special oral transmission,
overpowering many Tirthikas,
this precious doctrine of the Practice Lineage,
spreading like a blazing torch,
You are famous as Tilopa,
I pray to that Guru!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-3

Scorning all suffering,
You performed twelve great austerities
and supremely pleased Tilopa.
You were filled with Holy Dharma like a vase,
You gained the siddhi of the Dakini’s Rainbow Body
You are famous as Jnana Siddhi!
I pray to that Guru!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-4

Glorious Hevajra,
perfectly seeing all beings with compassion,
Emanating HUNG from your heart,
Famed as the Teacher from Mar, Dharma Mati,
You filled the realm of Tibet with Dharma!
I pray to that Guru!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-5

Accepted by that Venerable One,
inserting prana-mind into the central channel,
realizing all of whatever arises to be Dharmata,
Omnisciently, clairvoyantly knowing the minds of others,
You are known as Vajra Victory Banner
I pray to the Great Father Repa!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-6

Holder of the prophecy of the Great Sage of the Shakyas
Born near the region of Nyal,
You completed the study of healing and medicine,
and joined the two rivers of Kadampa and Mahamudra
holding both the lineages.
You are famous as the “World Renowned!”
I pray to that Guru!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-7

Accepted by that incomparable one,
you were able to emanate one billion forms
in a single night
Bound by the commitment of divine powers and so on,
You are famous as Dorje Gyalpo (Phagmodrupa)!
I pray to that Guru!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-8

Bhagavan Protector Akshobhya himself,
In order to establish many beings in happiness,
You guide myself and all other beings
Your famous signs are extremely difficult to describe
You are famous as Venerable Supreme Guide Tashi Pal!
I pray to that Father Guru!
Father, quickly hold with your compassion! ^11-9

The unmistaken precious lineage
of the glorious Taklung Kagyu
Held by the Venerable Holy Lord,
All those of the True Lineage, I supplicate you!
Venerable one, please grant your blessings! ^11-10

I pray that all the Gurus’ compassionate,
blessing may abide in my mind!
I pray to all the Yidams!
Send down ordinary and supreme accomplishments like rain! ^11-11

I pray to all the Dharmapalas!
Destroy all the masses of Tirthika Maras!
I pray to all the Wealth Deities!
Bestow the accomplishment of the jewel of all desirables! ^11-12

Beginning with this virtue,
May the precious Doctrine of the Buddha
and it’s essence, this Doctrine of the Practice Lineage,
continually pervade and expand
in all directions, times and countries!
May the doctrine holders’ bodies and lives remain stable, and
may all beings act in accord with Dharma! ^11-13

<small>The Yogin of Hevajra wrote this for the benefit of Gegpay Dorje. It is said that by hearing this supplication just one time, the blessing enters one’s mind and it is impossible to be separated from the Kagyu. So, then, fold your hands above your head and hold this in mind. After I prayed for this extremely extensive supplication, I felt that I didn’t need any other prayers. It is the blessing of inseparability. So, if you ask what my name is, I am called Shakya Trashi Kirti Ratna Sherab Pal Zangpo Dud de Choklay Namgyal. When I was staying at Rinpoche’s seat, I asked how the Seat could grow and flourish, and he said “you must care for this Seat and embrace it continually. This growing and flourishing may take almost as long as Lokeshvara’s commitment.” Then I asked if it would be appropriate to tell this to others, and he replied “very appropriate!” Then Kirti Ratna Su Siddhi also asked: “Please tell all the faithful ones!” “Teaching those without samaya is a great commitment.
